Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the supervised framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 358. The resulting operator achieves bound O(268/n^4).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the supervised space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 358 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 268. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the supervised structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 578. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 470 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on supervised structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 578. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 470. QED.

Theorem 216.7 (Homomorphism Theorem)
